                                left: b.center ? "50%" : "clamp(20px, 6vw, 84px)",
                                top: b.center ? "50%" : "auto",
                                bottom: b.center ? "auto" : "clamp(64px, 13vh, 130px)",
                                maxWidth: b.center ? "86%" : "min(560px, 80%)",
                                textAlign: b.center ? "center" : "left",
                                opacity: 0,
                                transform: b.center ? "translate(-50%,-50%)" : "translate3d(0,14px,0)",
                                color: "#F7F4EF",
                                pointerEvents: "none",
                                willChange: "opacity, transform",
                            }}
                        >
                            {!b.center && (
                                <div
                                    style={{
                                        display: "flex",
                                        alignItems: "center",
                                        gap: 12,
                                        fontSize: 10,
                                        letterSpacing: "0.28em",
                                        textTransform: "uppercase",
                                        color: "#C2A57C",
                                        marginBottom: 14,
                                        textShadow: "0 1px 14px rgba(0,0,0,.6)",
                                    }}
                                >
                                    <span style={{ display: "block", width: 26, height: 1, background: "currentColor", opacity: 0.6 }} />
                                    {b.eyebrow}
                                </div>
                            )}
                            <div
                                style={{
                                    fontFamily: '"Cormorant Garamond", "Times New Roman", Georgia, serif',
                                    fontWeight: 300,
                                    fontSize: b.center ? "clamp(52px, 10vw, 140px)" : "clamp(32px, 5vw, 72px)",
                                    fontStyle: b.center ? "italic" : "normal",
                                    lineHeight: 0.98,
                                    letterSpacing: b.center ? "-0.03em" : "-0.018em",
                                    textWrap: "balance" as any,
                                    textShadow: "0 2px 34px rgba(0,0,0,.42)",
                                }}
                            >
                                {b.head}
                            </div>
                            {b.sub && (
                                <div
                                    style={{
                                        marginTop: 16,
                                        maxWidth: "38ch",
                                        marginLeft: b.center ? "auto" : 0,
                                        marginRight: b.center ? "auto" : 0,
                                        fontFamily: '"Plus Jakarta Sans", ui-sans-serif, system-ui, sans-serif',
                                        fontWeight: 300,
                                        fontSize: "clamp(12px, 1.1vw, 14px)",
                                        lineHeight: 1.7,
                                        color: "rgba(247,244,239,.74)",
                                        textShadow: "0 1px 18px rgba(0,0,0,.55)",
                                    }}
                                >
                                    {b.sub}
                                </div>
                            )}
                        </div>
                    ))}
                </div>
            )}
            {status !== "ready" && (
                <div
                    style={{
                        position: "absolute",
                        inset: 0,
                        display: "flex",
                        alignItems: "center",
                        justifyContent: "center",
                        color: "rgba(247,244,239,0.62)",
                        font: "500 12px/1.4 ui-sans-serif, system-ui, sans-serif",
                        letterSpacing: "0.14em",
                        textTransform: "uppercase",
                        pointerEvents: "none",
                    }}
                >
                    {status === "failed" ? "This device cannot display the walkthrough" : "Casa da Luz"}
                </div>
            )}
        </div>
    )
}

ShadestateWalkthrough.defaultProps = {
    approachHeadline: "Architecture with permanence.",
    approachSubtitle: "Stone, oak and bronze, drawn to hold their composure for a hundred summers.",
    entryHeadline: "Honest materials.",
    entrySubtitle: "Travertine laid as it was quarried, oak left to weather, bronze that will only darken.",
    interiorHeadline: "Room to breathe.",
    interiorSubtitle: "Eight metres to the ceiling, a gallery above it, and glass from floor to sky at both ends.",
    terraceHeadline: "Built to stay.",
    terraceSubtitle: "The rear wall slides away, and the house turns to face the water and the city beyond it.",
    showCopy: true,
    scrollDriven: true,
    autoplay: false,
    startStop: "approach",
    speed: 1,
    loop: true,
    timeOfDay: "morning",
    cloudCover: 0.55,
    grassDensity: 1,
    waterRipples: 1,
    palms: true,
    city: true,
    quality: "auto",
    pausedOffscreen: true,
    maxDpr: 1.5,
    sectionHeight: 0,
}
